Schema is locked.\n3. **Push** the spike branch; do NOT merge.\n\n## Phase 3: Validator + mutation verbs + cursor + inbox\n\n### Step 7: Single-entry validator (`astrid/core/task/validator.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** `validator.py` exporting `validate_mutation(prior, proposed, *, lease_epoch_actual, lease_epoch_expected) -> None` raising `MutationInvariantError(invariant_id, element, reason)`.\n2. **All six invariants** against the proposed effective tree:\n   - I1 schema (re-run `_validate_plan`), I2 sibling-id uniqueness, I3 produces-ref integrity (no dangling refs to tombstoned/deleted steps), I4 adapter validation (`manual` requires non-empty `command` per SD-B; `remote-artifact` accepted at schema, rejected at runtime), I5 repeat.for_each.from_ref resolves, I6 lease-epoch CAS.\n3. **Stable invariant-id enum** so verbs return non-zero with `f\"validator: {err.invariant_id}: {err.reason}\"`.\n\n### Step 8: Plan-mutation verbs (`astrid/core/task/plan_verbs.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** `plan_verbs.py` exporting `cmd_plan_add_step`, `cmd_plan_edit_step`, `cmd_plan_remove_step`, `cmd_plan_supersede_step`.\n2. **Each verb**: load `plan.json` + replay `plan_mutated` events → current tree; compute proposed tree; `validate_mutation` under writer_epoch CAS; emit one `plan_mutated` event via `append_event_locked` (`events.py:92`) carrying `{actor, writer_epoch, op, diff, timestamp}`.\n3. **Dispatched-step gates**: edit/remove on dispatched → rejected at verb; remove on undispatched → tombstone event; supersede without `--scope` → argparse rejects via `choices=[\"all\",\"future-iterations\",\"future-items\"]`.\n4. **Group-step supersede semantics** (SD-L / correctness-4): scope=future-iterations on a mid-iteration group with dispatched children — the new v<N+1> children replace v<N> on the next iteration; children inherit by living under the new version. Document explicitly in verb docstring + test in `test_cursor_supersede_scopes.py`.\n5. **Effective-tree replay**: shared helper `apply_mutations(plan: TaskPlan, events: list[dict]) -> TaskPlan` used by verbs, cursor, and inbox.\n\n### Step 9: Cursor honoring versions + tombstones + scopes (`astrid/core/task/gate.py`, `astrid/core/task/lifecycle.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Extend the event-derived cursor** to `(step_id, step_version, dispatch_event_hash)` (SD-H). No new on-disk file; computed from `events.jsonl` + applied mutation events.\n2. **Walk** the effective plan respecting tombstones (skip), scope=all (cursor jumps to v<N+1> iter/item 1), scope=future-iterations / future-items (current iteration/item finishes against old version, next uses new).\n3. **`astrid status`** surfaces the cursor's current step at its current version (`lifecycle.cmd_status`).\n4. **Now flip `step_dir_for_path`'s `step_version` parameter to required** (callers-1) — all callers resolve via cursor state.\n\n### Step 10: Inbox path+version routing + identity-on-inbox (`astrid/core/task/inbox.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Rewrite** `_parse_entry` and `consume_inbox_entry` so entries match on `(plan_step_path, step_version, item_id?)`. New schema bumps a `schema_version` key on inbox JSON; absence is treated as legacy (handled by migration in Step 18a).\n2. **Identity enforcement parity** (callers-3): inbox entries for manual-adapter completion MUST carry `submitted_by` AND `submitted_by_kind: \"agent\" | \"actor\"`. Entries missing identity are rejected to `.rejected/` with a clear reason — identity enforcement does NOT leak through the inbox path.\n3. **Stale entries** (targeting tombstoned or fully-superseded steps) → `.rejected/<sha256>` via existing `_move_to`. **No entry vanishes silently** — every entry lands in `inbox/`, `.consumed/`, or `.rejected/` (stop-line).\n\n## Phase 4: Adapters wired into `cmd_next`\n\n### Step 11: Local adapter (`astrid/core/adapter/local.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **`dispatch`**: spawn subprocess in project root, return `DispatchResult{pid, started_at}`.\n2. **`complete`**: exit-0 + produces checks → `CompleteResult{status:\"completed\", returncode:0}`; non-zero or missing produces → `CompleteResult{status:\"failed\", reason, returncode}`. Cost typically absent (omitted, not null — SD-E).\n3. **Subprocess-outlives-tab**: dispatch is detach-capable; `astrid attach` + `astrid next` re-reads run state from events + inbox and resumes without the original parent.\n\n### Step 12: Manual adapter (`astrid/core/adapter/manual.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **`dispatch`** writes the `command` (+ optional `instructions` from SD-G) payload to `runs/<run>/steps/<id>/v<N>/dispatch.json` (SD-C) and returns `DispatchResult` empty of pid (manual work is out-of-band).\n2. **`complete`** has two paths:\n   - sync via `astrid ack` → identity carried on the ack event;\n   - async via inbox JSON → `consume_inbox_entry` enforces `submitted_by_kind ∈ {agent, actor}` (callers-3).\n\n### Step 13: Remote-artifact adapter (`astrid/core/adapter/remote_artifact.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **`dispatch`** raises with the exact deferral string: `astrid start / astrid next: step '<id>' declares adapter 'remote-artifact'; not yet implemented (Sprint 5a). Use --adapter local or manual.` `cmd_next` catches and exits non-zero. **Schema accepts the value; only runtime rejects.**\n\n### Step 14: Wire `cmd_next` to dispatch through adapters; emission ownership (`astrid/core/task/lifecycle.py`, `astrid/core/task/gate.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Replace** the hard-coded subprocess assumption in `cmd_next` with adapter resolution from `step.adapter`.\n2. **Single emission point** (SD-I / FLAG-S3-008): `cmd_next` calls `Adapter.dispatch` then emits `step_dispatched` via `append_event_locked`. On `Adapter.complete`'s return, `gate.record_dispatch_complete` emits the single `step_completed` / `step_failed` event with optional `cost`. Adapters never call `append_event_locked`.\n3. **`make_step_dispatched_event` extension** (SD-J / correctness-5): signature becomes `make_step_dispatched_event(plan_step_path, command, *, adapter=None, step_version=None, pid=None)`. Old events stay hash-stable; new events carry the extra keys. Replay reads with `.get(\"adapter\")`.\n4. **`requires_ack` enforcement**: adapter completion alone is insufficient; cursor stays on the step until an `astrid ack` event arrives with non-stale `writer_epoch`. Group steps with `requires_ack=true` block on the group's path until ack arrives (SD-B).\n5. **Cost**: `step_completed`/`step_failed` events include `cost` only when declared; omitted otherwise (SD-E).\n\n## Phase 5: Claim verb + ack identity + CLI registration\n\n### Step 15: Claim / unclaim (`astrid/core/task/claim.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Create** `claim.py` with `cmd_claim` / `cmd_unclaim`. Argparse: `astrid claim <step> [--for human:<name>] [--for agent:<id>]`. Default `--for` = current session's identity.\n2. **Block** read-only sessions with a clear error.\n3. **Emit** `claim` / `unclaim` events via `append_event_locked` under epoch CAS.\n\n### Step 16: Tighten `cmd_ack` argparse + caller audit (`astrid/core/task/lifecycle_ack.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Flip** `parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()` at `lifecycle_ack.py:122` to `required=True`. Neither and both both rejected at argparse non-zero.\n2. **Caller audit** (callers-2): grep all `astrid ack` invocations across `astrid/packs/`, `tests/`, and `astrid/core/task/hook.py`. Rewrite any that omit `--agent`/`--actor`; document any that intentionally rely on lax behavior (none expected — but verify before flipping).\n3. **Carry** `writer_epoch` from lease read into the synthesised event; reject stale acks.\n\n### Step 17: Register verbs in `pipeline.py`\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Extend** `LIFECYCLE_VERBS` (`pipeline.py:31`) with `plan`, `claim`, `unclaim`.\n2. **Add dispatch** branches in `_dispatch` mirroring `runs` / `hook` patterns (`_dispatch_plan_verbs` for the four mutation sub-verbs only — SD-D).\n3. **Update** the help block in `_print_entrypoint_help`.\n\n## Phase 6: Migrations (plan, step-paths, inbox)\n\n### Step 18: Plan migration (`scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_plans.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** `migrate_plans.py` (shebang, `chmod +x`, `--dry-run` default, `--apply` commits).\n2. **Walk** `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/` (configurable). For each `plan.json`:\n   - `code` → `adapter:\"local\"`, `command` preserved, `version:1`, `requires_ack:false`, `assignee:\"system\"`,\n   - `attested` → `adapter:\"manual\"`, `command` preserved, **`instructions` mapped verbatim** (SD-G / FLAG-S3-002), `version:1`, `requires_ack:true`, `assignee` per SD-A (`agent`→`any-agent`, `actor`→`any-human`),\n   - `nested` → group step with `children` from the nested plan; `produces` aggregated from descendants; no `command`/`instructions`; `version:1`.\n   - **Bump `TaskPlan.version` 1 → 2** (SD-F).\n3. **Idempotent**: detect already-migrated plans by `version == 2`; no-op cleanly.\n4. **Diff log**: every migration writes a sibling `plan.migration.log` documenting decisions and any TODO-flagged ambiguities.\n\n### Step 18a: Inbox-entry migration (`scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_inbox.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **NEW** — addresses FLAG-S3-005 / scope-2. Walks `runs/*/inbox/*.json` and rewrites legacy `{step_id: ...}` entries to `{plan_step_path: <derived>, step_version: 1, schema_version: 2, ...}`. The path is derived from `plan.json`'s step tree by ID lookup; ambiguous IDs (with sibling collisions) are moved to `.rejected/` with a clear reason rather than silently routed.\n2. **Idempotent**: entries with `schema_version: 2` are no-ops.\n3. **`--dry-run` default**.\n\n### Step 19: Step-path migration (`scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_step_paths.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** the script. Walk `runs/*/steps/<id>/*`, rename to `runs/*/steps/<id>/v1/*`. Iterations/items move under `v1/`.\n2. **Idempotent**: skip directories already containing a `v<N>/` child.\n3. **No-op path**: empty workspace exits 0 cleanly.\n\n## Phase 7: Tests\n\n### Step 20: Kernel schema + mutation tests (`tests/task/`)\n**Scope:** Large\n1. **`test_plan_schema_collapsed.py`** — collapsed `Step` validates; leaf-vs-group XOR; `requires_ack` allowed on group (SD-B); `instructions` accepted as optional (SD-G); six invariants positive + negative.\n2. **`test_plan_mutation_verbs.py`** — add/edit/remove/supersede happy paths; post-dispatch edit/remove rejected; supersede without `--scope` rejected at argparse.\n3. **`test_step_versioning.py`** — supersede creates `v2/`, `v1/` survives, cursor tracks current version.\n4. **`test_cursor_supersede_scopes.py`** — all three scopes under `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each`; **group-step supersede children inheritance** (SD-L / correctness-4) explicit case.\n5. **`test_inbox_path_version_match.py`** — entries route across supersede; stale → `.rejected/`; no entry vanishes; **missing `submitted_by_kind` rejects** (callers-3).\n6. **`test_claim_unclaim.py`** — events emitted; read-only blocked.\n7. **`test_ack_identity_required.py`** — argparse rejects no-identity and both-supplied.\n8. **`test_step_dispatched_event_compat.py`** (NEW, correctness-5) — old `step_dispatched` events without `adapter`/`step_version`/`pid` still hash-replay stably alongside new events.\n\n### Step 21: Adapter tests (`tests/adapter/`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **`__init__.py`** + scaffolding.\n2. **`test_local.py`** — dispatch, completion modes, failure modes, subprocess-outlives-tab.\n3. **`test_manual.py`** — dispatch payload at `v<N>/dispatch.json` (SD-C); ack-driven AND inbox-driven completion; **inbox-driven completion missing `submitted_by_kind` rejected** (callers-3).\n4. **`test_remote_artifact_rejected.py`** — `astrid next` prints exact deferral message and exits non-zero.\n\n### Step 22: Migration tests (`tests/migrations/sprint-3/`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **`test_plan_migration.py`** — all three old kinds collapsing; **`AttestedStep.instructions` preserved end-to-end** (FLAG-S3-002); `TaskPlan.version: 1 → 2` (SD-F); `--dry-run` vs `--apply`; idempotence (re-run on already-v2 is no-op).\n2. **`test_inbox_migration.py`** (NEW) — legacy `step_id`-only entries rewritten; `schema_version: 2` becomes the idempotence discriminator; ambiguous-ID entries routed to `.rejected/` (FLAG-S3-005).\n3. **`test_step_path_migration.py`** — `steps/<id>/*` → `steps/<id>/v1/*`; idempotence; no-op path.\n\n### Step 23: Rewrite or delete legacy kernel tests\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Rewrite or delete** `tests/test_task_kernel_attested.py`, `tests/test_task_kernel_nested.py`, `tests/test_task_plan_schema.py`, `tests/test_step_dir_path.py`.\n2. **Verify** `tests/test_task_kernel_dispatch.py`, `tests/test_task_kernel_e2e.py`, `tests/test_task_kernel_events.py`, `tests/test_task_kernel_gate.py`, `tests/test_task_repeat_*` still pass after the schema swap. Adjust call-site fixtures.\n3. **Verify** Sprint 0/1/2 full suite stays green modulo the 7 known S2 pre-existing failures.\n\n## Phase 8: Docs\n\n### Step 24: Async-completion worked examples (`docs/reshape/sprint-3/async-completion.md`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Local-subprocess-outlives-tab** worked example.\n2. **Manual out-of-band ack via inbox** worked example — explicitly showing `submitted_by_kind` requirement.\n\n### Step 25: Hype-spike findings (`docs/reshape/sprint-3/hype-spike-findings.md`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Document** ≥3 gaps + the schema revisions landed in response (drafted in Phase 2; finalised here).\n\n## Execution Order\n\n1. **Phase 0** decisions locked first (no code, but the schema/event-shape contracts ride on these).\n2. **Phase 1** (schema draft + path helpers + adapter ABC stub + full call-site sweep including `orchestrate/cli.py`, `orchestrate/test_runner.py`, `project/run.py:81`).\n3. **Phase 2** (hype spike) — must compile through orchestrate/* refactor first.\n4. **Phase 3** (validator + mutation verbs + cursor + inbox + identity-on-inbox).         ### Suggested Approach
        Four phases. **Phase A — schema + kernel:** (1) Rewrite plan.py to a single collapsed Step dataclass (id, version, adapter, requires_ack, assignee, produces, repeat, command?, children?, cost?, superseded_by?), keeping _assert_unique_paths. (2) Add astrid/core/task/validator.py with one validate_mutation function running all six invariants and returning structured (invariant_id, element) errors. (3) Update step-path helpers across astrid/core/task/ to v<N>/iterations|items pattern. (4) Write scripts/migrations/sprint-3/{migrate_plans.py,migrate_step_paths.py} with --dry-run default; exercise idempotence against ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/. **Phase B — adapters:** (5) Create astrid/core/adapter/{__init__,local,manual,remote_artifact}.py with a common ABC shaped to cmd_next's current flow (dispatch / poll / complete). (6) Wire cmd_next in lifecycle.py to route by adapter, emit step_dispatched event, record optional cost on completion events. **Phase C — mutation/identity verbs:** (7) plan_verbs.py implements add/edit/remove/supersede — every verb routes through validate_mutation, emits one plan_mutated event via append_event_locked under writer_epoch CAS. (8) Rewrite inbox.py keying on (path, version, item_id); stale → .rejected/. (9) claim.py — claim/unclaim verbs blocking read-only sessions. (10) Tighten cmd_ack argparse: exactly one of --agent/--actor required; carry writer_epoch; reject stale. (11) Register new verbs in astrid/pipeline.py (LIFECYCLE_VERBS + dispatch table). **Phase D — tests + spike + docs:** (12) Write the new test files as failing specs against the new surfaces, then iterate to green. (13) Migrate / rewrite legacy kernel tests (attested, nested, plan_schema, step_dir_path) for the collapsed schema. (14) Branch reshape/sprint-3-hype-spike, port first 3 hype steps (transcribe/cut/render), enumerate gaps in docs/reshape/sprint-3/hype-spike-findings.md, fold schema revisions back into sprint-3 main branch BEFORE locking. Spike branch pushed but never merged. (15) Write docs/reshape/sprint-3/async-completion.md with worked examples for local subprocess-outlives-tab and manual out-of-band ack. Honor stop-lines if triggered (especially during spike). Use astrid plan add-step to dogfood tracking sub-tasks of this sprint itself.
        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
# Sprint 3 — Step model overhaul, mutable plans, location-agnostic execution

Astrid reshape sprint 3. Full design: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/idea.md` § Sprint 3 (lines 214–295). This brief expands the sprint section for execution. **This is a large sprint and the architectural keystone of the reshape — premium/robust/high profile picked deliberately.**

Working directory: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid`. Start from branch `reshape/sprint-2`; create `reshape/sprint-3` off it and work there.

## Goal

Collapse the step model into one shape (no more `code`/`attested`/`nested` kinds). Make plans editable mid-run with explicit mutation rules. Make execution location-agnostic via three adapters (only `local` and `manual` ship this sprint; `remote-artifact` is reserved and rejected at runtime). Add the `claim` verb. Land the kernel-level pieces (validator, versioned step paths, mutation events, inbox path+version matching) that Sprint 4 + 5a all depend on.

**No orchestrator porting (Sprint 5a/5b territory).** Sprint 3 is the kernel + the adapters; existing packs are migrated forward mechanically.

## Foundational decisions (locked, do not re-debate)

### The new Step shape

```
Step {
  id: str
  version: int                       # bumped when a dispatched step is superseded
  adapter: "local" | "manual" | "remote-artifact"
  requires_ack: bool
  assignee: "system" | "agent:<id>" | "any-agent" | "human:<name>" | "any-human"
  produces: [{name, path, check}]
  repeat: until | for_each | none
  command: str?                      # leaf step: the thing to run; interpreted by adapter
  children: [Step]?                  # group step: nesting; replaces old `nested` kind
  cost: {amount, currency, source}?  # recorded at completion
  superseded_by: {
    to_version: int,
    scope: "all" | "future-iterations" | "future-items"  # REQUIRED; no default
  }?
}
```

- Leaf step has `command`, no `children`.
- Group step has `children`, no `command`. Its `produces` aggregates from descendants.
- `code` / `attested` / `nested` step kinds are **gone** (migrated forward — see Migration).
- `version` starts at 1 for the initial step; bumps to 2+ on supersede.

### Supersession scope is mandatory

A `supersede-step` verb without an explicit `scope` is rejected at the verb. Three scopes:
- `"all"` — aborts in-flight iterations/items and restarts at iter/item 1 of the new version.
- `"future-iterations"` — `repeat.until` only; current iteration finishes against old version, iter+1 uses new version.
- `"future-items"` — `repeat.for_each` only; current item finishes against old version, next item uses new version.

### Plan mutation rules

- `plan.json` is the *initial* plan, written once at run start.
- The *effective* plan = `plan.json` + replayed mutation events from `events.jsonl`.
- **Append-only once dispatched.** A step is "dispatched" the moment `astrid next` returns its payload (event: `step_dispatched`). After dispatch:
  - `remove-step` is rejected. Use `abort` on the run instead, or `supersede-step`.
  - `edit-step` is rejected. Use `supersede-step` to bump version.
  - The original `steps/<id>/v1/` directory is **never deleted**.
- **Undispatched steps** can be added, edited, or removed freely. `remove-step` writes a tombstone event; the cursor skips tombstoned steps.
- **Canonical step path is always versioned**: every step lives at `steps/<id>/v<N>/...`. Iterations and items live under `steps/<id>/v<N>/iterations/NNN/` and `steps/<id>/v<N>/items/<item-id>/`.

### The six plan-mutation invariants

Single function (call it `validate_mutation` or similar) runs **all six** invariants against the proposed effective tree:

1. **Schema validation** — full re-validate of the proposed effective tree (not a diff).
2. **Sibling-id uniqueness** at every frame (existing `plan.py:_assert_unique_paths` rule, applied to effective tree).
3. **`produces` reference integrity** — no dangling refs to deleted/tombstoned steps.
4. **Adapter validation** — declared adapter is `local | manual | remote-artifact`, command shape matches adapter (e.g. `manual` cannot have empty `command` string).
5. **Repeat-source validation** — `repeat.for_each.from_ref` resolves to a real prior `produces` in the effective tree.
6. **Lease epoch CAS** — the writer's epoch matches current (Sprint 1's lease).

Mutations that fail **any** check are rejected at the verb. No partial application. The verb returns a non-zero exit with the specific invariant that failed.

### Three adapters; only two ship this sprint

- **`local`** — `command` is a shell command run as a subprocess in the project root. Outputs land in `produces/`. Completion = exit code 0 + produces checks pass.
- **`manual`** — agent (or human) runs the work out-of-band; `command` is the dispatch payload (instructions). The agent is responsible for landing outputs in `produces/`. Completion = `astrid ack` (sync) or completion JSON in inbox (async).
- **`remote-artifact`** — schema-reserved here, **rejected at runtime** in Sprint 3 with: `astrid start / astrid next: step '<id>' declares adapter 'remote-artifact'; not yet implemented (Sprint 5a). Use --adapter local or manual.` Full implementation lands in Sprint 5a alongside the hype port.

Adapters are first-class code, not config.

### Ack identity at ack time, not author time

`astrid ack <step> --decision {approve,retry,iterate,abort} --agent <id>|--actor <name> [--evidence <path>]`. Both `--agent` and `--actor` are required (one or the other) — **no anonymous acks**. Every ack carries the `writer_epoch` it read; stale acks are rejected by the kernel.

### Authoring split

- **Leaf-template** — what we used to call an executor. One concrete unit of work. Same pack layout on disk (`pack/<name>/run.py` + `executor.yaml`).
- **Plan-template** — what we used to call an orchestrator: a reusable composition. Materializes into a step subtree at plan-build or plan-mutation time.

Runtime never sees templates — only the resulting step tree. During Sprint 3, the new template kinds land **alongside** the legacy `OrchestratorDefinition` YAML — both readable. Legacy YAML is materialized into the new step tree on the way in. (Sprint 5b removes legacy YAML acceptance.)

### Capture-everything rule

Anything producing an audit-worthy artifact = a step. The friction is small: `astrid plan add-step --command '...' --produces '...'` + `astrid next`. The alternative — agent runs python ad-hoc, artifact appears with no provenance — is what we're eliminating. Truly ephemeral commands don't need it.

## Deliverables

### Step schema + plan rewrite

- Rewrite `astrid/core/task/plan.py` against the new step shape. Keep `_assert_unique_paths` (or its equivalent in the new code) and the validators for the existing invariants; add what's needed for the new ones.
- Versioned step path everywhere: `steps/<id>/v<N>/...`, `steps/<id>/v<N>/iterations/NNN/`, `steps/<id>/v<N>/items/<item-id>/`. Update all path helpers in `astrid/core/task/`.
- **`code` / `attested` / `nested` step kinds are gone.** Migration script (see below) rewrites existing plans.

### Plan-mutation verbs + event

- `astrid plan add-step` — insert a step into the effective tree at a given path. Position via `--after <step-path>` / `--before <step-path>` / `--into <group-path>`.
- `astrid plan edit-step` — modify an undispatched step (fields specifiable as flags).
- `astrid plan remove-step` — write a tombstone event for an undispatched step. Rejected on dispatched steps.
- `astrid plan supersede-step` — bump version on a dispatched (or any) step. **Requires `--scope {all,future-iterations,future-items}`.**
- All four verbs emit a single `plan_mutated` event in `events.jsonl` carrying: actor, writer_epoch, diff (the specific operation), timestamp. The hash chain extends to plan mutations.

### Plan-mutation validator

- Single entry point that runs all six invariants. Every verb routes through it. Rejection is total.
- Returns structured error: which invariant failed + the specific element that triggered it.
- Test: each invariant has at least one positive (passes) and one negative (rejects with the right error code) case.

### Cursor

- Cursor stores `(step_id, step_version, dispatch_event_hash)` per dispatched step.
- Walks the effective plan respecting tombstones and supersedes (honoring `scope` per repeat semantics).
- `astrid status` shows the cursor's current step at its current version.

### Inbox path+version match

- Rewrite `astrid/core/task/inbox.py` so entries match on `(plan_step_path, step_version, item_id?)` not just the leaf step id.
- Inbox entries from before a supersede event route to the original version's directory; entries after route to the new version.
- Stale entries that target a tombstoned or fully-superseded step move to `.rejected/` (existing pattern — extend it).

### Adapters

- `astrid/core/adapter/local.py` — subprocess-based local execution. Dispatch = spawn subprocess (sync or detached); completion = exit code 0 + produces checks; failure = non-zero exit OR missing produces.
- `astrid/core/adapter/manual.py` — emit dispatch payload (`command` field) to the agent's display path; completion = `astrid ack` event OR completion JSON arriving in inbox.
- `astrid/core/adapter/remote_artifact.py` — stub that rejects at dispatch time with the deferral message above.
- All three implement a common interface (e.g. `Adapter.dispatch(step, run_ctx)` / `Adapter.poll(step, run_ctx)` / `Adapter.complete(step, run_ctx)`). Pick the interface to fit how `astrid next` already works.

### Claim verb

- `astrid claim <step> [--for human:<name>] [--for agent:<id>]`. Default `--for` = current session's identity.
- Emits `claim` / `unclaim` events.
- Read-only sessions blocked from claiming.

### `requires_ack` enforcement

- Steps with `requires_ack: true` cannot complete on adapter completion alone; require an `astrid ack` event with a non-stale `writer_epoch`.
- `--agent` or `--actor` is **required** at ack time. Argparse rejects neither/both.

### Mutating verbs include `writer_epoch` + CAS

- Every mutating verb reads the current lease's `writer_epoch` before mutating; bumps it as part of the mutation; rejects if the epoch advanced under it.
- Relies on Sprint 1's lease primitives directly — do NOT reimplement.

### Cost field on completion events

- Each step completion event (`step_completed`, `step_failed`) carries `cost: {amount, currency, source}` if the adapter or executing code declared one.
- Adapters MAY emit cost; local subprocess execution typically won't. The field is optional but the schema reserves it (Sprint 4 + 5a depend on this).

### Inbox-driven async-completion documented + exercised

- Worked example for `local` adapter: subprocess that outlives the tab. Demonstrate that closing the tab, opening a new one, `astrid attach`, `astrid next` picks up wherever inbox left things.
- Worked example for `manual` adapter: out-of-band ack landing as a completion JSON in inbox.
- Documentation lives at `docs/reshape/sprint-3/async-completion.md`.

### Migrations

- **Plan migration** — every existing `plan.json` rewritten to the collapsed schema. Every existing step gets `adapter: local` and `version: 1`. `code` / `attested` / `nested` step kinds rewritten to the new shape (best-effort mapping; ambiguous cases get `adapter: local` + a TODO note in the diff log).
- **Step path migration** — every existing `steps/<id>/...` directory becomes `steps/<id>/v1/...` in one shot. Run on `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/` (which is currently mostly empty — but the script must be idempotent and exercise the no-op path cleanly).
- Both migrations live under `scripts/migrations/sprint-3/`. `--dry-run` default; `--apply` commits.

### Hype port spike — back half of sprint, throwaway, do NOT merge to reshape/sprint-3

- Scratch branch `reshape/sprint-3-hype-spike` off `reshape/sprint-3`.
- Take the first three steps of `builtin.hype` only (transcribe → cut → render) and port them against the new step model.
- Deliverable is `docs/reshape/sprint-3/hype-spike-findings.md` enumerating every schema gap, adapter mismatch, and cost-field ambiguity discovered.
- **Schema revisions resulting from the spike land in Sprint 3, not deferred to a follow-up.** Revise the schema while it's still cheap.
- The spike branch is throwaway — push it for archival but do not merge.

### Tests

- `tests/task/test_plan_schema_collapsed.py` — new step schema validation, including all six invariants positive + negative.
- `tests/task/test_plan_mutation_verbs.py` — add/edit/remove/supersede each succeed in the happy path and reject correctly when post-dispatch / scope-missing / invariant-failing.
- `tests/task/test_step_versioning.py` — supersede produces `v2/`, `v1/` survives, cursor tracks correct version.
- `tests/task/test_cursor_supersede_scopes.py` — all three scopes exercised under repeat.until and repeat.for_each.
- `tests/task/test_inbox_path_version_match.py` — entries route correctly across a supersede event; stale entries land in `.rejected/`.
- `tests/adapter/test_local.py` — subprocess dispatch, completion, failure modes.
- `tests/adapter/test_manual.py` — dispatch payload emission, ack-driven completion, inbox-driven completion.
- `tests/adapter/test_remote_artifact_rejected.py` — `astrid next` rejects cleanly with the deferral message.
- `tests/task/test_claim_unclaim.py` — claim emits event, unclaim emits event, read-only session blocked.
- `tests/task/test_ack_identity_required.py` — argparse rejects no-agent-no-actor; both rejected too.
- `tests/migrations/test_plan_migration.py` — collapsed-schema rewrite across all old step kinds.
- `tests/migrations/test_step_path_migration.py` — `steps/<id>/...` → `steps/<id>/v1/...`, idempotence, no-op path.
- All Sprint 0 + Sprint 1 + Sprint 2 tests still pass (regression — full pytest must come back green-modulo-known-pre-existing-failures).

## Stop-lines

1. **Hype spike reveals plan-template materialization can't express hype's dynamic discovery** (or any step shape is fundamentally wrong for hype). Halt before locking the Sprint 3 schema; redesign rather than push through. Write the redesign to `docs/reshape/sprint-3/schema-redesign.md` and stop for human review.
2. **Apex contract (S1) regresses under plan mutation events**. Locked event-append + epoch CAS is what plan-mutated rides on; if multi-tab races appear, halt and fix S1's primitives, not S3's surface.
3. **Inbox path+version routing loses entries silently**. If any inbox entry vanishes (not routed, not rejected, just gone) under any supersede or tombstone path, that's a stop-line — entries must always have a deterministic destination.

## Out of scope (do NOT touch)

- The `remote-artifact` adapter implementation (Sprint 5a).
- Orchestrator porting — hype proper, event_talks, thumbnail_maker (Sprint 5a/5b). The hype spike is a throwaway port of the first three steps only.
- RunPod lift (Sprint 4).
- Anything in `astrid/packs/builtin/human_review/`, `astrid/packs/seinfeld/` — pre-existing WIP, leave untouched.
- Export / verify / cost aggregation (Sprint 5b).
- `astrid projects timeline` (the legacy reigh-app subcommand) — leave it alone in this sprint; rename happens in Sprint 5b.

## Acceptance criteria

- `git branch --show-current` returns `reshape/sprint-3`.
- All Sprint 0 + Sprint 1 + Sprint 2 tests still green (modulo the 7 pre-existing unrelated failures verified in S2 — those are out of scope).
- `pytest tests/task/ tests/adapter/ tests/migrations/sprint-3/ -v` all green.
- Plan-mutation validator: every invariant has positive + negative test coverage.
- `astrid plan add-step / edit-step / remove-step / supersede-step` round-trip end-to-end on a freshly-created run.
- `astrid plan supersede-step` without `--scope` is rejected at argparse.
- `astrid plan edit-step` / `remove-step` on a dispatched step is rejected at the verb.
- `astrid plan remove-step` on an undispatched step writes a tombstone event; cursor skips it.
- `astrid claim` / `astrid unclaim` emit events; read-only session is blocked.
- `astrid ack` with neither `--agent` nor `--actor` is rejected at argparse.
- `local` adapter: subprocess that outlives the tab, reattach, `astrid next` picks up.
- `manual` adapter: dispatch + ack-driven completion + inbox-driven completion all work.
- `remote-artifact` adapter: rejected at `astrid next` with the deferral message.
- Hype spike: `docs/reshape/sprint-3/hype-spike-findings.md` exists with at least 3 enumerated gaps + the schema revisions that landed in response.
- Migrations: `scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_plans.py --dry-run` and `--apply` both clean against `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/`. `scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_step_paths.py` likewise.

## Useful repo references

- `astrid/core/task/plan.py` — current step schema; will be rewritten.
- `astrid/core/task/events.py` — `append_event_locked` from Sprint 1; reuse for `plan_mutated`, `claim`, `unclaim`.
- `astrid/core/task/inbox.py` — current inbox; rewrite for path+version matching.
- `astrid/core/session/lease.py` — Sprint 1's lease with `writer_epoch`. CAS lives here; don't reimplement.
- `astrid/core/timeline/` — Sprint 2's package; do not modify in this sprint.
- `astrid/packs/builtin/hype/` — the spike's target; do NOT modify the real pack, only port to a scratch branch.
- `idea.md` § Sprint 3 (lines 214–295) — canonical sprint definition.
- `idea.md` § Foundation > Load-bearing decisions — the why behind these decisions.

## Style notes

- Type hints + dataclasses.
- Pathlib over os.path.
- Migration scripts: shebang, `chmod +x`, `--dry-run` default.
- All new modules include a one-line docstring.
- New tests follow existing test naming + structure conventions.
- For new event kinds (`plan_mutated`, `claim`, `unclaim`, `step_dispatched`): use `append_event_locked` from S1 — every emit is hash-chained and lease-CAS-protected.
- For supersede scope: model the three values as a string Literal (or enum), not a bare string. Argparse rejects unknown values.

## Megaplan-specific guidance

- This sprint is profile `premium/robust/high` because: (1) the kernel pieces here are downstream-cascading — every later sprint depends on these contracts; (2) the six-invariant validator is exactly the sort of contract that benefits from extra critique passes; (3) the hype spike loop has real architectural risk that wants high-depth planning.
- If the hype spike surfaces a stop-line, halt the sprint rather than push through. Document the gap and stop for human review.
- Use `astrid plan add-step` for dogfooding any sub-tasks of this sprint that are worth tracking — friction felt here is friction every user feels.

        Current plan (markdown):

# Implementation Plan: Sprint 3 — Step model overhaul, mutable plans, location-agnostic execution

## Overview

Sprint 3 collapses the Astrid step model into a single `Step` shape, introduces mid-run plan-mutation verbs gated by a six-invariant validator, versioned step paths (`steps/<id>/v<N>/…`), three execution adapters (`local` + `manual` ship; `remote-artifact` rejects), the `claim` verb, ack identity enforcement, optional `cost` on completion events, and inbox path+version routing. Branch `reshape/sprint-3` is already checked out clean; Sprint 0/1/2 surfaces are frozen.

This revision resolves 22 critique flags from iteration 1. The structure (8 phases, hype-spike-before-schema-lock) is unchanged. Specifics now pinned down:

- **Schema-level discriminator**: `TaskPlan.version` bumps **1 → 2** at migration time, giving the migration script a clean idempotence discriminator (FLAG-S3-004 / correctness-1).
- **Attested instructions destination**: the collapsed `Step` adds optional `instructions: str | None` field; manual-adapter migration maps legacy `AttestedStep.instructions` here verbatim (FLAG-S3-002 / correctness-2).
- **Call-site sweep extended**: explicitly enumerates `astrid/orchestrate/cli.py`, `astrid/orchestrate/test_runner.py`, `astrid/core/task/__init__.py:70,116-117` (re-exports), `astrid/core/project/run.py:81`. (`astrid/core/orchestrator/*` does NOT import the step kinds — the critique overstated; verified by grep.) (FLAG-S3-003 / scope-1 / all_locations-1).
- **Inbox-entry migration**: new `scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_inbox.py` rewrites legacy `step_id`-only entries to `(plan_step_path, step_version=1)` before the schema bump activates (FLAG-S3-005 / scope-2).
- **Cursor persistence**: explicitly **event-derived** (read from `events.jsonl`'s hash chain — no new on-disk artifact). No additional locking primitives needed (FLAG-S3-007 / scope-3).
- **Completion-event ownership**: `Adapter.complete()` returns a result struct; `cmd_next` (via `gate.record_dispatch_complete`) emits the **single** `step_completed`/`step_failed` event. Adapters never emit events directly (FLAG-S3-008 / all_locations-2).
- **Event-factory backward compat**: `make_step_dispatched_event` extends with optional `adapter`, `step_version`, `pid` fields. Old events stay hash-chain-stable because canonical-JSON key-sorted output of an old payload is unchanged by adding new keys to NEW payloads (correctness-5).
- **`_reject_orchestrators_run` fate**: kept as `adapter == "local"` guard only; manual adapter bypasses (manual commands legitimately ARE `astrid orchestrators run ...` payloads for agent inspection) (correctness-3).
- **Group-step supersede children inheritance**: when a group is superseded with scope=future-iterations, children of v<N+1> are used for subsequent iterations — children inherit by living under the new version (correctness-4).
- **Sequencing fix**: `step_dir_for_path` accepts `step_version: int = 1` default first (sprint-1-compat shim during Phase 1), then flips to required AFTER cursor record + migrations land in Phase 3/6 (callers-1).
- **Ack-caller audit**: explicit step to grep all `astrid ack` invocations across `astrid/packs/`, tests, and hooks; rewrite/document any that omit identity flags (callers-2).
- **Manual-inbox identity contract**: inbox entries for manual-adapter completion MUST carry `submitted_by` AND `submitted_by_kind: "agent" | "actor"` — identity enforcement parity with `astrid ack` (callers-3).
- **Five open metadata questions resolved by settled decisions in Phase 0** (FLAG-S3-001 / issue_hints).
- **Criteria quality**: the manual `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/` dry-run demoted from `must` to `should` (it's manual local verification not runnable in CI) (criteria_quality).

## Phase 0: Settled decisions (schema-and-event determinism locked before Phase 1 schema work)

These resolve the five open metadata questions from iteration 1 (FLAG-S3-001 / issue_hints). Phase 1 builds on these; they are not re-debated.

- **SD-A — Assignee mapping at migration time**: legacy `AttestedStep.ack.kind="agent"` → `assignee="any-agent"`. `ack.kind="actor"` → `assignee="any-human"`. (Both preserve anonymity; concrete agent/actor pinning happens at claim-time via the new `claim` verb.) Migration diff log records every mapping; ambiguous cases get `assignee="any-agent"` + a TODO in the diff log.
- **SD-B — Group-step `requires_ack` is allowed**. A group step with `requires_ack=true` completes only when (a) all children are complete AND (b) a non-stale ack event arrives for the group's path. Validator accepts `requires_ack` on group steps; adapter dispatch is a no-op for group steps (they don't have a `command`).
- **SD-C — Manual-adapter dispatch payload lands at `runs/<run>/steps/<id>/v<N>/dispatch.json`** (the per-step versioned directory). Session-scoped display surfaces are Sprint 5a territory. `astrid attach` re-renders pending dispatches by scanning `dispatch.json` files under the current cursor's path.
- **SD-D — `plan` verb dispatch table is minimal**: only the four mutation sub-verbs (`add-step`, `edit-step`, `remove-step`, `supersede-step`) wire in `pipeline.py`. The `_dispatch_plan_verbs` router is shape-neutral so adding `plan show` / `plan validate` later is a one-line registration; no namespace pre-reservation now.
- **SD-E — `cost` field is omitted from event payloads when undeclared** (NOT serialised as `null`). canonical-event-JSON determinism prefers absence to null for optional fields; this matches existing event-shape conventions in `events.py`. The schema reserves the key; consumers read with `.get("cost")`.
- **SD-F — `TaskPlan.version` bumps to 2**. The new schema rejects load of `version != 2`. Migration scripts set `version: 2` on rewrite; the value itself is the idempotence discriminator (no migration if `version == 2`).
- **SD-G — `instructions: str | None = None` added to `Step`**. Optional field carrying agent-facing prose, used primarily by the manual adapter. Migration maps legacy `AttestedStep.instructions` here verbatim.
- **SD-H — Cursor is event-derived, no new on-disk artifact**. The cursor's `(step_id, step_version, dispatch_event_hash)` is reconstructed by walking `events.jsonl` + applying `plan_mutated` events on top of `plan.json`. No new file, no new lock.
- **SD-I — Completion-event ownership**: adapters' `complete()` returns a `CompleteResult{status, cost?}` struct; the single `step_completed` or `step_failed` event is emitted by `cmd_next` (via `gate.record_dispatch_complete`) on the back of that return value. Adapters do NOT call `append_event_locked` themselves.
- **SD-J — Event-factory backward compat**: `make_step_dispatched_event` signature becomes `(plan_step_path, command, *, adapter=None, step_version=None, pid=None)`. Old events (pre-S3) remain hash-stable; canonical-JSON sorting means adding new optional keys to NEW payloads cannot retroactively change OLD payload hashes.
- **SD-K — `_reject_orchestrators_run` applies only when `adapter == "local"`**. Manual adapter intentionally bypasses (its `command` is a payload, not an argv; legitimate manual payloads may include `astrid orchestrators run ...` for agent inspection).
- **SD-L — Group-step supersede inheritance**: when a group is superseded, its `children` under v<N+1> are the new definition; scope semantics apply at the group level and children are accessed via the new version on the next iteration/item.

## Phase 1: Draft schema + path helpers + adapter ABC stub (the keystone, unlocked)

### Step 1: Audit current step model and inbox routing (`astrid/core/task/plan.py`, `astrid/core/task/inbox.py`, `astrid/core/task/gate.py`, `astrid/orchestrate/cli.py`, `astrid/orchestrate/test_runner.py`, `astrid/core/task/__init__.py`, `astrid/core/project/run.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** the current step union (`astrid/core/task/plan.py:17,57,86`) and external references: `iter_steps_with_path` (`plan.py:155`), `step_dir_for_path` (`plan.py:115`), `_validate_*_step` family, `_assert_unique_paths` (`plan.py:556`), `_reject_orchestrators_run` (`plan.py:530`).
2. **Enumerate every importer** of `CodeStep | AttestedStep | NestedStep | TaskPlanStep` (verified by grep — only 7 files):
   - `astrid/core/task/plan.py` (rewrite),
   - `astrid/core/task/lifecycle.py`, `lifecycle_ack.py`, `gate.py`, `inbox.py` (call-site updates),
   - **`astrid/orchestrate/cli.py:26-28` + `:142,150,394,398,404,434`** (isinstance branches → dispatch on `step.adapter`),
   - **`astrid/orchestrate/test_runner.py:40-41` + `:131,153`** (same).
3. **Enumerate `step_dir_for_path` callers** beyond the task package:
   - `astrid/core/task/__init__.py:70,116-117` (re-exports — pass-through, no body to update),
   - **`astrid/core/project/run.py:11,81`** — `step_dir_for(project_slug, parent_run_id, step_id, root=projects_root)`. Add `step_version=1` default during Phase 1; flip to required after cursor record lands in Phase 3.
4. **Confirm** `astrid/core/orchestrator/*` does NOT import the step kinds (grep result: clean). No changes there.
5. **Map** the cursor's current contract — `gate.peek_current_step` is event-derived from `events.jsonl`. Confirm the new `(step_id, step_version, dispatch_event_hash)` model stays event-derived (SD-H); no new on-disk artifact.

### Step 2: Draft the collapsed `Step` dataclass (`astrid/core/task/plan.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** the three step dataclasses with one frozen `Step` per idea.md + SD-G:
   - `id: str`, `version: int = 1`, `adapter: Literal["local","manual","remote-artifact"]`,
   - `requires_ack: bool = False`, `assignee: str = "system"`,
   - `produces: tuple[ProducesEntry, ...] = ()`, `repeat: Repeat | None = None`,
   - `command: str | None = None`, `instructions: str | None = None` (SD-G),
   - `children: tuple[Step, ...] | None = None`,
   - `cost: CostEntry | None = None`, `superseded_by: SupersededRef | None = None`.
2. **`__post_init__` invariants**:
   - `command XOR children` (leaf has command, group has children) — group steps may carry `requires_ack` (SD-B) but no `command`/`instructions`.
   - `assignee` parser validates the five forms (`system | agent:<id> | any-agent | human:<name> | any-human`).
3. **Bump `TaskPlan.version` to 2** (SD-F): `_validate_plan` rejects `version != 2`. This is the migration discriminator.
4. **Preserve** `_assert_unique_paths`, `parse_from_ref`, `iter_steps_with_path` (rewritten over `Step.children`), the `produces` dict validator, and repeat validators.
5. **Scope `_reject_orchestrators_run` to `adapter == "local"`** (SD-K). Manual adapter skips the guard.
6. **Mark** the schema as *draft* in the module docstring until the hype spike has fed back.

### Step 3: Versioned step paths with phased call-site rollout (`astrid/core/task/plan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `step_dir_for_path` to accept `step_version: int = 1` (default for the duration of Phase 1; flipped to required after Phase 3 cursor record + Phase 6 migrations land — see callers-1). Path becomes `steps/<id>/v<N>/[iterations/NNN | items/<item-id>]`.
2. **Sweep callers**: `gate.py:711,999,1010,1133`, `lifecycle.py:594`, `lifecycle_ack.py`, `inbox.py:207`, `events.py`, **`astrid/core/project/run.py:81`** (re-exported via `astrid/core/task/__init__.py:70`). Each call passes `step_version` from cursor state; default-=1 falls through during the migration window.

### Step 4: Adapter ABC + result types (`astrid/core/adapter/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `astrid/core/adapter/__init__.py` with `Adapter` Protocol shaped to `cmd_next`'s flow:
   - `dispatch(step: Step, run_ctx: RunContext) -> DispatchResult`
   - `poll(step: Step, run_ctx: RunContext) -> PollResult`
   - `complete(step: Step, run_ctx: RunContext) -> CompleteResult` — returns `CompleteResult{status: "completed" | "failed", returncode?: int, cost?: CostEntry, reason?: str}` (SD-I).
2. **Adapters never emit events directly** (SD-I). `cmd_next` reads `Adapter.complete()`'s return and calls `append_event_locked` once.
3. **Stub** `local.py`, `manual.py`, `remote_artifact.py` with `NotImplementedError` placeholders for the spike to exercise.

## Phase 2: Hype spike (open the loop early; lock the schema only after)

### Step 5: Throwaway spike branch (`reshape/sprint-3-hype-spike`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Branch** `reshape/sprint-3-hype-spike` off the current `reshape/sprint-3` HEAD. Do NOT modify `astrid/packs/builtin/hype/` on `reshape/sprint-3`.
2. **Verify** the orchestrate/cli + orchestrate/test_runner refactor from Step 1 compiles before the spike starts (their `isinstance` branches now dispatch on `step.adapter`). If they don't compile, the spike can't even import — fix Step 1 before proceeding (FLAG-S3-003 mitigation).
3. **Port** the first three `builtin.hype` steps (transcribe → cut → render) to the draft `Step` shape. Fill `adapter`, `assignee`, `requires_ack`, `instructions`, `produces` per their current YAML contract.
4. **Exercise** the adapter touchpoints against the stub interface.
5. **Enumerate** every gap discovered in `docs/reshape/sprint-3/hype-spike-findings.md` (≥3 gaps).
6. **Stop-line check**: if dynamic discovery cannot be expressed, halt, write `docs/reshape/sprint-3/schema-redesign.md`, stop for human review.

### Step 6: Fold spike revisions back into `reshape/sprint-3` (`astrid/core/task/plan.py`, `astrid/core/adapter/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Cherry-pick** schema additions, adapter interface revisions, validator-invariant clarifications.
2. **Remove** the *draft* marker on `plan.py`. Schema is locked.
3. **Push** the spike branch; do NOT merge.

## Phase 3: Validator + mutation verbs + cursor + inbox

### Step 7: Single-entry validator (`astrid/core/task/validator.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `validator.py` exporting `validate_mutation(prior, proposed, *, lease_epoch_actual, lease_epoch_expected) -> None` raising `MutationInvariantError(invariant_id, element, reason)`.
2. **All six invariants** against the proposed effective tree:
   - I1 schema (re-run `_validate_plan`), I2 sibling-id uniqueness, I3 produces-ref integrity (no dangling refs to tombstoned/deleted steps), I4 adapter validation (`manual` requires non-empty `command` per SD-B; `remote-artifact` accepted at schema, rejected at runtime), I5 repeat.for_each.from_ref resolves, I6 lease-epoch CAS.
3. **Stable invariant-id enum** so verbs return non-zero with `f"validator: {err.invariant_id}: {err.reason}"`.

### Step 8: Plan-mutation verbs (`astrid/core/task/plan_verbs.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `plan_verbs.py` exporting `cmd_plan_add_step`, `cmd_plan_edit_step`, `cmd_plan_remove_step`, `cmd_plan_supersede_step`.
2. **Each verb**: load `plan.json` + replay `plan_mutated` events → current tree; compute proposed tree; `validate_mutation` under writer_epoch CAS; emit one `plan_mutated` event via `append_event_locked` (`events.py:92`) carrying `{actor, writer_epoch, op, diff, timestamp}`.
3. **Dispatched-step gates**: edit/remove on dispatched → rejected at verb; remove on undispatched → tombstone event; supersede without `--scope` → argparse rejects via `choices=["all","future-iterations","future-items"]`.
4. **Group-step supersede semantics** (SD-L / correctness-4): scope=future-iterations on a mid-iteration group with dispatched children — the new v<N+1> children replace v<N> on the next iteration; children inherit by living under the new version. Document explicitly in verb docstring + test in `test_cursor_supersede_scopes.py`.
5. **Effective-tree replay**: shared helper `apply_mutations(plan: TaskPlan, events: list[dict]) -> TaskPlan` used by verbs, cursor, and inbox.

### Step 9: Cursor honoring versions + tombstones + scopes (`astrid/core/task/gate.py`, `astrid/core/task/lifecycle.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend the event-derived cursor** to `(step_id, step_version, dispatch_event_hash)` (SD-H). No new on-disk file; computed from `events.jsonl` + applied mutation events.
2. **Walk** the effective plan respecting tombstones (skip), scope=all (cursor jumps to v<N+1> iter/item 1), scope=future-iterations / future-items (current iteration/item finishes against old version, next uses new).
3. **`astrid status`** surfaces the cursor's current step at its current version (`lifecycle.cmd_status`).
4. **Now flip `step_dir_for_path`'s `step_version` parameter to required** (callers-1) — all callers resolve via cursor state.

### Step 10: Inbox path+version routing + identity-on-inbox (`astrid/core/task/inbox.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `_parse_entry` and `consume_inbox_entry` so entries match on `(plan_step_path, step_version, item_id?)`. New schema bumps a `schema_version` key on inbox JSON; absence is treated as legacy (handled by migration in Step 18a).
2. **Identity enforcement parity** (callers-3): inbox entries for manual-adapter completion MUST carry `submitted_by` AND `submitted_by_kind: "agent" | "actor"`. Entries missing identity are rejected to `.rejected/` with a clear reason — identity enforcement does NOT leak through the inbox path.
3. **Stale entries** (targeting tombstoned or fully-superseded steps) → `.rejected/<sha256>` via existing `_move_to`. **No entry vanishes silently** — every entry lands in `inbox/`, `.consumed/`, or `.rejected/` (stop-line).

## Phase 4: Adapters wired into `cmd_next`

### Step 11: Local adapter (`astrid/core/adapter/local.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`dispatch`**: spawn subprocess in project root, return `DispatchResult{pid, started_at}`.
2. **`complete`**: exit-0 + produces checks → `CompleteResult{status:"completed", returncode:0}`; non-zero or missing produces → `CompleteResult{status:"failed", reason, returncode}`. Cost typically absent (omitted, not null — SD-E).
3. **Subprocess-outlives-tab**: dispatch is detach-capable; `astrid attach` + `astrid next` re-reads run state from events + inbox and resumes without the original parent.

### Step 12: Manual adapter (`astrid/core/adapter/manual.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`dispatch`** writes the `command` (+ optional `instructions` from SD-G) payload to `runs/<run>/steps/<id>/v<N>/dispatch.json` (SD-C) and returns `DispatchResult` empty of pid (manual work is out-of-band).
2. **`complete`** has two paths:
   - sync via `astrid ack` → identity carried on the ack event;
   - async via inbox JSON → `consume_inbox_entry` enforces `submitted_by_kind ∈ {agent, actor}` (callers-3).

### Step 13: Remote-artifact adapter (`astrid/core/adapter/remote_artifact.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`dispatch`** raises with the exact deferral string: `astrid start / astrid next: step '<id>' declares adapter 'remote-artifact'; not yet implemented (Sprint 5a). Use --adapter local or manual.` `cmd_next` catches and exits non-zero. **Schema accepts the value; only runtime rejects.**

### Step 14: Wire `cmd_next` to dispatch through adapters; emission ownership (`astrid/core/task/lifecycle.py`, `astrid/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** the hard-coded subprocess assumption in `cmd_next` with adapter resolution from `step.adapter`.
2. **Single emission point** (SD-I / FLAG-S3-008): `cmd_next` calls `Adapter.dispatch` then emits `step_dispatched` via `append_event_locked`. On `Adapter.complete`'s return, `gate.record_dispatch_complete` emits the single `step_completed` / `step_failed` event with optional `cost`. Adapters never call `append_event_locked`.
3. **`make_step_dispatched_event` extension** (SD-J / correctness-5): signature becomes `make_step_dispatched_event(plan_step_path, command, *, adapter=None, step_version=None, pid=None)`. Old events stay hash-stable; new events carry the extra keys. Replay reads with `.get("adapter")`.
4. **`requires_ack` enforcement**: adapter completion alone is insufficient; cursor stays on the step until an `astrid ack` event arrives with non-stale `writer_epoch`. Group steps with `requires_ack=true` block on the group's path until ack arrives (SD-B).
5. **Cost**: `step_completed`/`step_failed` events include `cost` only when declared; omitted otherwise (SD-E).

## Phase 5: Claim verb + ack identity + CLI registration

### Step 15: Claim / unclaim (`astrid/core/task/claim.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `claim.py` with `cmd_claim` / `cmd_unclaim`. Argparse: `astrid claim <step> [--for human:<name>] [--for agent:<id>]`. Default `--for` = current session's identity.
2. **Block** read-only sessions with a clear error.
3. **Emit** `claim` / `unclaim` events via `append_event_locked` under epoch CAS.

### Step 16: Tighten `cmd_ack` argparse + caller audit (`astrid/core/task/lifecycle_ack.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Flip** `parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()` at `lifecycle_ack.py:122` to `required=True`. Neither and both both rejected at argparse non-zero.
2. **Caller audit** (callers-2): grep all `astrid ack` invocations across `astrid/packs/`, `tests/`, and `astrid/core/task/hook.py`. Rewrite any that omit `--agent`/`--actor`; document any that intentionally rely on lax behavior (none expected — but verify before flipping).
3. **Carry** `writer_epoch` from lease read into the synthesised event; reject stale acks.

### Step 17: Register verbs in `pipeline.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Extend** `LIFECYCLE_VERBS` (`pipeline.py:31`) with `plan`, `claim`, `unclaim`.
2. **Add dispatch** branches in `_dispatch` mirroring `runs` / `hook` patterns (`_dispatch_plan_verbs` for the four mutation sub-verbs only — SD-D).
3. **Update** the help block in `_print_entrypoint_help`.

## Phase 6: Migrations (plan, step-paths, inbox)

### Step 18: Plan migration (`scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_plans.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `migrate_plans.py` (shebang, `chmod +x`, `--dry-run` default, `--apply` commits).
2. **Walk** `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/` (configurable). For each `plan.json`:
   - `code` → `adapter:"local"`, `command` preserved, `version:1`, `requires_ack:false`, `assignee:"system"`,
   - `attested` → `adapter:"manual"`, `command` preserved, **`instructions` mapped verbatim** (SD-G / FLAG-S3-002), `version:1`, `requires_ack:true`, `assignee` per SD-A (`agent`→`any-agent`, `actor`→`any-human`),
   - `nested` → group step with `children` from the nested plan; `produces` aggregated from descendants; no `command`/`instructions`; `version:1`.
   - **Bump `TaskPlan.version` 1 → 2** (SD-F).
3. **Idempotent**: detect already-migrated plans by `version == 2`; no-op cleanly.
4. **Diff log**: every migration writes a sibling `plan.migration.log` documenting decisions and any TODO-flagged ambiguities.

### Step 18a: Inbox-entry migration (`scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_inbox.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **NEW** — addresses FLAG-S3-005 / scope-2. Walks `runs/*/inbox/*.json` and rewrites legacy `{step_id: ...}` entries to `{plan_step_path: <derived>, step_version: 1, schema_version: 2, ...}`. The path is derived from `plan.json`'s step tree by ID lookup; ambiguous IDs (with sibling collisions) are moved to `.rejected/` with a clear reason rather than silently routed.
2. **Idempotent**: entries with `schema_version: 2` are no-ops.
3. **`--dry-run` default**.

### Step 19: Step-path migration (`scripts/migrations/sprint-3/migrate_step_paths.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** the script. Walk `runs/*/steps/<id>/*`, rename to `runs/*/steps/<id>/v1/*`. Iterations/items move under `v1/`.
2. **Idempotent**: skip directories already containing a `v<N>/` child.
3. **No-op path**: empty workspace exits 0 cleanly.

**Scope:** Small

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 0** decisions locked first (no code, but the schema/event-shape contracts ride on these).
2. **Phase 1** (schema draft + path helpers + adapter ABC stub + full call-site sweep including `orchestrate/cli.py`, `orchestrate/test_runner.py`, `project/run.py:81`).
3. **Phase 2** (hype spike) — must compile through orchestrate/* refactor first.
4. **Phase 3** (validator + mutation verbs + cursor + inbox + identity-on-inbox). Cursor record lands here; **then** `step_dir_for_path`'s `step_version` flips to required.
5. **Phase 4** (adapters + `cmd_next` wiring; single-emission-point ownership; backward-compat event factory).
6. **Phase 5** (claim + ack tightening + pipeline registration).
7. **Phase 6** (plan + inbox + step-path migrations).
8. **Phase 7** (tests).
9. **Phase 8** (docs).

## Validation Order

1. **Targeted unit tests** per module as it lands: `pytest tests/task/test_plan_schema_collapsed.py -v`, then validator, mutation verbs, cursor, inbox.
2. **Compatibility test**: `pytest tests/task/test_step_dispatched_event_compat.py -v` (old + new event hash-chain replay).
3. **Adapter tests**: `pytest tests/adapter/ -v`.
4. **Migration tests against synthetic fixtures**: `pytest tests/migrations/sprint-3/ -v` (the CI-runnable verification — `must`-criterion).
5. **Manual dry-run** against `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/` (`should`-criterion — manual local check; tree is mostly empty per the brief).
6. **Full task suite**: `pytest tests/task/ tests/adapter/ tests/migrations/sprint-3/ -v`.
7. **Regression**: whole-repo pytest, expecting green modulo S2's 7 known pre-existing failures.
8. **End-to-end on a fresh run**: `astrid attach`, plan add/edit/remove/supersede round-trip; `astrid claim` / `unclaim`; ack argparse rejection; remote-artifact rejection at `astrid next`.
9. **Stop-line guards**: confirm no inbox entry vanishes; confirm S1 apex contract unmoved (concurrency tests under `tests/concurrency/` re-run after Phase 3 lands).

      "reason": "TaskPlan.version bumps 1 \u2192 2 (SD-F). Schema rejects load of version != 2. The value itself is the migration's idempotence discriminator."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-S3-005",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Added Step 18a `migrate_inbox.py` plus test_inbox_migration.py \u2014 rewrites legacy step_id-only entries to (plan_step_path, step_version=1, schema_version=2). Idempotent on already-v2 entries. Ambiguous-ID entries routed to .rejected/ with explicit reason (no silent loss)."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-S3-007",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Cursor is explicitly event-derived (SD-H); no new on-disk artifact, no new locking primitive. Stays adjacent to Sprint 1's apex contract without disturbing it."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-S3-008",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Adapter.complete() returns a CompleteResult struct (SD-I); cmd_next/gate.record_dispatch_complete emits the single step_completed/step_failed event. Adapters never call append_event_locked. No double-emit, no missed event."
    },
    {
      "id": "issue_hints",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as FLAG-S3-001 \u2014 all five questions resolved in Phase 0 settled decisions, not deferred to assumptions."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-1",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as FLAG-S3-004 \u2014 TaskPlan.version 1 \u2192 2 gives a clean before/after discriminator for the migration script."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-2",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as FLAG-S3-002 \u2014 `instructions: str | None` field added to Step; migration preserves AttestedStep.instructions verbatim."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-3",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "_reject_orchestrators_run scoped to adapter=='local' (SD-K). Manual adapter intentionally bypasses since manual commands legitimately include orchestrators run payloads for agent inspection. Documented in Step 2."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-4",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Group-step supersede children-inheritance semantics specified (SD-L) \u2014 scope semantics apply at group level; children of v<N+1> replace v<N> on next iteration/item. Explicit test in test_cursor_supersede_scopes.py."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-5",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "make_step_dispatched_event signature extended with optional adapter/step_version/pid keys (SD-J). canonical_event_json's key-sorted output means old events stay hash-stable; replay reads with .get(). Added test_step_dispatched_event_compat.py to enforce."
    },
    {
      "id": "scope-1",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as FLAG-S3-003 \u2014 astrid/orchestrate/cli.py and astrid/orchestrate/test_runner.py enumerated in Phase 1 Step 1. Verified astrid/core/orchestrator/* does NOT need touching (grep clean)."
    },
    {
      "id": "scope-2",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as FLAG-S3-005 \u2014 explicit migrate_inbox.py script addresses in-flight queued acks."
    },
    {
      "id": "scope-3",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as FLAG-S3-007 \u2014 cursor stays event-derived; no S1-apex-adjacent locking parity work needed."
    },
    {
      "id": "all_locations-1",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Phase 1 Step 1 explicitly enumerates astrid/core/task/__init__.py:70,116-117 (re-exports) and astrid/core/project/run.py:11,81 (the orchestrator child-run setup caller). Both are part of the call-site sweep."
    },
    {
      "id": "all_locations-2",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same as FLAG-S3-008 \u2014 SD-I locks emission ownership to cmd_next/gate.record_dispatch_complete only."
    },
    {
      "id": "callers-1",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Sequencing rewritten: step_dir_for_path takes `step_version: int = 1` default through Phase 1-2; the required-version flip is deferred to Phase 3 Step 9 AFTER the cursor record lands. No ordering risk."
    },
    {
      "id": "callers-2",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Phase 5 Step 16 adds an explicit caller-audit step that greps astrid/packs/, tests/, and astrid/core/task/hook.py for `astrid ack` invocations before flipping required=True. Any lax-relying callsite is rewritten or documented."
    },
    {
      "id": "callers-3",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Manual-adapter inbox completion entries MUST carry submitted_by AND submitted_by_kind: 'agent' | 'actor' (Phase 3 Step 10, Phase 4 Step 12, Phase 7 Steps 20.5 + 21.3). Identity enforcement contract is the same on inbox as it is on `astrid ack` \u2014 no leak path."
    },
    {
      "id": "criteria_quality",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Demoted '~/Documents/reigh-workspace/astrid-projects/ dry-run + apply clean' criterion from must to should \u2014 it requires manual local execution not runnable in CI. The synthetic-fixture-based test_plan_migration / test_inbox_migration / test_step_path_migration tests remain must."
    }
  ],

  "assumptions": [
    "Phase 0 settled decisions (SD-A through SD-L) are authoritative for the remainder of Phase 1-8 work; they reflect the resolution of all five open metadata questions plus the schema/event-determinism choices forced by the critique. They are not re-debated.",
    "TaskPlan.version bumps 1 \u2192 2 in the new schema. Pre-S3 plan.json files load only via the migration script; the live schema rejects version != 2. This is the migration's idempotence discriminator.",
    "Inbox JSON gains a `schema_version: 2` key. Entries without the key are treated as legacy and handled by migrate_inbox.py before the new schema activates. Migrated entries become v2 and idempotent.",
    "Adapter.complete() returns a CompleteResult struct; the SINGLE step_completed/step_failed event is emitted by cmd_next/gate.record_dispatch_complete from that return. No adapter calls append_event_locked directly.",
    "Cursor is event-derived from events.jsonl + applied plan_mutated events; no new on-disk artifact, no new lock, no parity work with Sprint 1's lease.",
    "make_step_dispatched_event's optional-keyword extension (adapter, step_version, pid) is hash-chain-stable: canonical_event_json sorts keys, so adding new optional fields to NEW payloads does not change OLD payload hashes. test_step_dispatched_event_compat.py enforces this invariant.",
    "_reject_orchestrators_run survives but applies only to adapter=='local'. Manual-adapter commands legitimately include `astrid orchestrators run ...` payloads as agent-facing instructions.",
    "Call-site sweep is complete after Phase 1 Step 1: 7 importers of legacy step kinds (4 in astrid/core/task/, 2 in astrid/orchestrate/, 1 in plan.py itself), plus 2 step_dir_for/step_dir_for_path callers outside the task package (project/run.py:81, task/__init__.py re-exports). astrid/core/orchestrator/* needs no touch (grep clean).",
    "Manual-adapter inbox completion enforces identity (submitted_by + submitted_by_kind) at the inbox parser. Same contract as astrid ack \u2014 no leak path.",
    "The hype spike branch reshape/sprint-3-hype-spike starts only AFTER Phase 1 Step 1's orchestrate/* call-site refactor compiles. Otherwise the spike fails at import before schema gaps can surface.",
    "Sprint 1/2 surfaces under astrid/core/timeline/, astrid/core/session/lease.py, and astrid/core/task/events.py:append_event_locked are reused verbatim. No edits to those modules' contracts."
  ],
  "delta_from_previous_percent": 67.12,
  "structure_warnings": []
}

        "priority": "must",
